which satisfiesr≤ρ∞/4r\leq\rho_{\infty}/4andK3​r≤μ∞/8K_{3}r\leq\mu_{\infty}/8. The same symbolrris used in every lemma below.

## Lemma S2.1.

LetG1,…,GK​∼iid​Gamma​(α,1)G_{1},\dots,G_{K}\overset{\mathrm{iid}}{\sim}\mathrm{Gamma}(\alpha,1)andY:=∑kGkY:=\sum_{k}G_{k}. Setuk=Gk/Yu_{k}=G_{k}/Y. Then(uk)k=1K∼Dirichlet​(α​𝟏K)(u_{k})_{k=1}^{K}\sim\text{Dirichlet}(\alpha\mathbf{1}_{K})with𝔼​uk=1/K{\mathbb{E}}u_{k}=1/KandCov⁡(uk,uℓ)=K​δk​l−1K2​(K​α+1),\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}(u_{k},u_{\ell})=\frac{K\delta_{kl}-1}{K^{2}(K\alpha+1)},

whereδk​l=1\delta_{kl}=1ifk=lk=land0otherwise.

## Proof.

The Gamma-Dirichlet construction and the independence of(Gk/Y)k(G_{k}/Y)_{k}fromYYare classical. With𝔼​uk=1/K{\mathbb{E}}u_{k}=1/K,𝔼​uk2=α+1K​(K​α+1){\mathbb{E}}u_{k}^{2}=\frac{\alpha+1}{K(K\alpha+1)}, and𝔼​uk​uℓ=αK​(K​α+1){\mathbb{E}}u_{k}u_{\ell}=\frac{\alpha}{K(K\alpha+1)}fork≠ℓk\neq\ell, subtracting1/K21/K^{2}gives the stated covariance.
∎

## Lemma S2.2.

LetE1,…,EK∼Gamma​(α,1)E_{1},\dots,E_{K}\sim\mathrm{Gamma}(\alpha,1)i.i.d. withα>0\alpha>0, setV:=∑k=1KEkV:=\sum_{k=1}^{K}E_{k}andζk:=Ek/V\zeta_{k}:=E_{k}/V. Then, for any fixed vectorsv1,…,vK∈ℝdv_{1},\dots,v_{K}\in{\mathbb{R}}^{d}with∑kvk=0\sum_{k}v_{k}=0, the centered linear formh:=∑k(ζk−1K)​vkh:=\sum_{k}(\zeta_{k}-\tfrac{1}{K})v_{k}satisfiesVh=∑k(Ek−α)vk=:MVh=\sum_{k}(E_{k}-\alpha)v_{k}=:Mandh⟂Vh\perp V, and hence𝔼​‖M‖4=𝔼​[V4]​𝔼​‖h‖4{\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}={\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]\,{\mathbb{E}}\|h\|^{4}.

## Proof.

The additivity of the Gamma distribution showsV∼Gamma​(K​α,1)V\sim\mathrm{Gamma}(K\alpha,1). To establish the independence we find the joint density of(ζ1,…,ζK−1,V)(\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K-1},V)via the change of variables from(E1,…,EK)(E_{1},\dots,E_{K}), noting thatζK\zeta_{K}is a deterministic function ofζ1,…,ζK−1\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K-1}.

The joint density of(E1,…,EK)(E_{1},\dots,E_{K})on(0,∞)K(0,\infty)^{K}isfE​(e)=Γ​(α)−K​(∏kekα−1)​e−∑kekf_{E}(e)=\Gamma(\alpha)^{-K}(\prod_{k}e_{k}^{\alpha-1})e^{-\sum_{k}e_{k}}. Pass to coordinates(ζ1,…,ζK−1,V)(\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K-1},V)through the bijectionek=V​ζke_{k}=V\zeta_{k}fork<Kk<KandeK=V​(1−∑j<Kζj)=V​ζKe_{K}=V(1-\sum_{j<K}\zeta_{j})=V\zeta_{K}, defined on the simplex{ζk>0,∑kζk=1}\{\zeta_{k}>0,\ \sum_{k}\zeta_{k}=1\}times(0,∞)(0,\infty). The derivative matrix∂(e1,…,eK)/∂(ζ1,…,ζK−1,V)\partial(e_{1},\dots,e_{K})/\partial(\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K-1},V)hasV​IK−1V\,I_{K-1}as its top-left(K−1)×(K−1)(K-1)\times(K-1)block from∂ek/∂ζj=V​1​{j=k}\partial e_{k}/\partial\zeta_{j}=V\,\mathbf{1}\{j=k\}, the vector(ζ1,…,ζK−1)⊤(\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K-1})^{\top}in the firstK−1K-1entries of its last column from∂ek/∂V=ζk\partial e_{k}/\partial V=\zeta_{k}, and last row(−V,…,−V,ζK)(-V,\dots,-V,\zeta_{K})from∂eK/∂ζj=−V\partial e_{K}/\partial\zeta_{j}=-Vand∂eK/∂V=ζK\partial e_{K}/\partial V=\zeta_{K}. The determinant is invariant under a row operation, so adding rows1,…,K−11,\dots,K-1to the last row turns it into(0,…,0,1)(0,\dots,0,1)and makes an upper triangular matrix with diagonal(V,…,V,1)(V,\dots,V,1), whose determinant isVK−1V^{K-1}. Hencefζ,V​(ζ,V)=1Γ​(α)K​(∏kζkα−1)​VK​α−1​e−V=Γ​(K​α)Γ​(α)K​∏kζkα−1⏟Dirichlet​(α​𝟏K)​density⋅VK​α−1​e−VΓ​(K​α)⏟Gamma​(K​α,1)​density.\displaystyle f_{\zeta,V}(\zeta,V)=\frac{1}{\Gamma(\alpha)^{K}}\Big(\prod_{k}\zeta_{k}^{\alpha-1}\Big)V^{K\alpha-1}e^{-V}=\underbrace{\frac{\Gamma(K\alpha)}{\Gamma(\alpha)^{K}}\prod_{k}\zeta_{k}^{\alpha-1}}_{\text{Dirichlet}(\alpha\mathbf{1}_{K})\text{ density}}\cdot\underbrace{\frac{V^{K\alpha-1}e^{-V}}{\Gamma(K\alpha)}}_{\mathrm{Gamma}(K\alpha,1)\text{ density}}.

Therefore‖M‖4=V4​‖h‖4\|M\|^{4}=V^{4}\|h\|^{4}withVVindependent of‖h‖4\|h\|^{4}, giving𝔼​‖M‖4=𝔼​[V4]​𝔼​‖h‖4{\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}={\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]\,{\mathbb{E}}\|h\|^{4}. Finally𝔼​[V4]=Γ​(K​α+4)/Γ​(K​α)=K​α​(K​α+1)​(K​α+2)​(K​α+3){\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]=\Gamma(K\alpha+4)/\Gamma(K\alpha)=K\alpha(K\alpha+1)(K\alpha+2)(K\alpha+3)sinceV∼Gamma​(K​α,1)V\sim\mathrm{Gamma}(K\alpha,1).
∎

## Lemma S2.3.

Conditional on(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi),Covw⁡(n​gDirichlet∣𝐗1:n)=nn+1​Σψ,n,Covu⁡(n​gBlock∣𝐗1:n,π)=nS​γ+1​Σ¯ψ,n,\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}_{w}(\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})=\frac{n}{n+1}\Sigma_{\psi,n},\qquad\operatorname{Cov}_{u}(\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Block}}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi)=\frac{n}{S\gamma+1}\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n},

and𝔼w​‖BDirichlet‖F2=1n+1​κn2{\mathbb{E}}_{w}\|B_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{1}{n+1}\kappa_{n}^{2},𝔼u​‖BBlock‖F2=1S​γ+1​κ¯n2{\mathbb{E}}_{u}\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{1}{S\gamma+1}\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}. Moreover, for a uniformly random balanced partition,𝔼π​[Σ¯ψ,n∣𝐗1:n]=S−1n−1​Σψ,n.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{S-1}{n-1}\Sigma_{\psi,n}.

## Proof.

The characterization below uses the first-order condition∑iψi=0\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0and its variants∑sψ¯s=1nS​∑iψi=0\sum_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}=\frac{1}{n_{S}}\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0and∑iAi=0\sum_{i}A_{i}=0.

## Per-record covariance.

By LemmaS2.1with(K,α)=(n,1)(K,\alpha)=(n,1),Cov⁡(wi,wj)=n​δi​j−1n2​(n+1)\operatorname{Cov}(w_{i},w_{j})=\frac{n\delta_{ij}-1}{n^{2}(n+1)}. SincegDirichlet=∑i(wi−1n)​ψig_{\text{Dirichlet}}=\sum_{i}(w_{i}-\frac{1}{n})\psi_{i}is linear in𝐰{\bf w}and shifting by the constant−1n-\frac{1}{n}does not change covariances,Covw⁡(gDirichlet)=∑i,jCov⁡(wi,wj)​ψi​ψj⊤=1n2​(n+1)​[n​∑iψi​ψi⊤−(∑iψi)​(∑jψj)⊤],\displaystyle\operatorname{Cov}_{w}(g_{\text{Dirichlet}})=\sum_{i,j}\operatorname{Cov}(w_{i},w_{j})\psi_{i}\psi_{j}^{\top}=\frac{1}{n^{2}(n+1)}\Big[n\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\psi_{i}^{\top}-\Big(\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\Big)\Big(\sum_{j}\psi_{j}\Big)^{\top}\Big],

where the second term vanishes because∑iψi=0\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0. HenceCovw⁡(gDirichlet)=1n​(n+1)​∑iψi​ψi⊤=1n+1​Σψ,n\operatorname{Cov}_{w}(g_{\text{Dirichlet}})=\frac{1}{n(n+1)}\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\psi_{i}^{\top}=\frac{1}{n+1}\Sigma_{\psi,n}, which also gives𝔼w​‖gDirichlet‖2=1n​Tr​Covw⁡(n​gDirichlet)=σψ,n2n+1{\mathbb{E}}_{w}\|g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\text{Tr}\operatorname{Cov}_{w}(\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Dirichlet}})=\frac{\sigma_{\psi,n}^{2}}{n+1}.

## Block covariance.

The block-constant reallocationw¯i=uπ​(i)/nS\bar{w}_{i}=u_{\pi(i)}/n_{S}gives, after grouping the indicesi∈Isi\in I_{s},gBlock=∑i(w¯i−1n)​ψi=∑sus​ψ¯s−1S​∑sψ¯s=∑s(us−1S)​ψ¯sg_{\text{Block}}=\sum_{i}(\bar{w}_{i}-\frac{1}{n})\psi_{i}=\sum_{s}u_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}-\frac{1}{S}\sum_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}=\sum_{s}(u_{s}-\frac{1}{S})\bar{\psi}_{s}. This is the same linear form as in the per-record case with(K,α)=(S,γ)(K,\alpha)=(S,\gamma)andψ¯s\bar{\psi}_{s}in place ofψi\psi_{i}, and∑sψ¯s=0\sum_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}=0plays the role of the first-order condition. HenceCovu⁡(n​gBlock)=nS​γ+1​Σ¯ψ,n\operatorname{Cov}_{u}(\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Block}})=\frac{n}{S\gamma+1}\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}and𝔼u​‖gBlock‖2=σ¯ψ,n2S​γ+1{\mathbb{E}}_{u}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}=\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}}{S\gamma+1}.

## Hessian second moments.

The sumsBBlock=∑s(us−1S)​A¯sB_{\text{Block}}=\sum_{s}(u_{s}-\frac{1}{S})\bar{A}_{s}andBDirichlet=∑i(wi−1n)​AiB_{\text{Dirichlet}}=\sum_{i}(w_{i}-\frac{1}{n})A_{i}have the same linear structure with the matricesA¯s\bar{A}_{s}andAiA_{i}as coefficients. Viewing matrices as vectors under the Frobenius inner product⟨M,M′⟩F=Tr​(M⊤​M′)\langle M,M^{\prime}\rangle_{F}=\text{Tr}(M^{\top}M^{\prime})and applying the per-record and block computations coordinatewise gives𝔼u​‖BBlock‖F2=1S​γ+1​1S​∑s‖A¯s‖F2=κ¯n2S​γ+1{\mathbb{E}}_{u}\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{1}{S\gamma+1}\frac{1}{S}\sum_{s}\|\bar{A}_{s}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}}{S\gamma+1}and𝔼w​‖BDirichlet‖F2=κn2n+1{\mathbb{E}}_{w}\|B_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|_{F}^{2}=\frac{\kappa_{n}^{2}}{n+1}, using∑sA¯s=0\sum_{s}\bar{A}_{s}=0and∑iAi=0\sum_{i}A_{i}=0.

## Partition mean.

For a uniformly random balanced partitionπ\pi, each indexiiis equally likely to occupy any of thennslots, soℙπ​(i∈Is)=nS/n=1S{\mathbb{P}}_{\pi}(i\in I_{s})=n_{S}/n=\frac{1}{S}. Giveni∈Isi\in I_{s}, a distinct indexjjoccupies one of the remainingn−1n-1slots, exactlynS−1n_{S}-1of which lie inIsI_{s}, soℙπ​({i,j}⊂Is)=1S​nS−1n−1{\mathbb{P}}_{\pi}(\{i,j\}\subset I_{s})=\frac{1}{S}\frac{n_{S}-1}{n-1}. Fromψ¯s​ψ¯s⊤=1nS2​∑i,j∈Isψi​ψj⊤=1nS2​∑i,j𝟏​{π​(i)=π​(j)=s}​ψi​ψj⊤\bar{\psi}_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}^{\top}=\frac{1}{n_{S}^{2}}\sum_{i,j\in I_{s}}\psi_{i}\psi_{j}^{\top}=\frac{1}{n_{S}^{2}}\sum_{i,j}\mathbf{1}\{\pi(i)=\pi(j)=s\}\psi_{i}\psi_{j}^{\top}and the identity∑i≠jψi​ψj⊤=(∑iψi)​(∑jψj)⊤−∑iψi​ψi⊤=−n​Σψ,n\sum_{i\neq j}\psi_{i}\psi_{j}^{\top}=\big(\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\big)\big(\sum_{j}\psi_{j}\big)^{\top}-\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\psi_{i}^{\top}=-n\Sigma_{\psi,n},𝔼π​[ψ¯s​ψ¯s⊤]=1nS2​[1S​∑iψi​ψi⊤+1S​nS−1n−1​∑i≠jψi​ψj⊤]=nS​nS2​(1−nS−1n−1)​Σψ,n=S−1n−1​Σψ,n,\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\psi}_{s}\bar{\psi}_{s}^{\top}]=\frac{1}{n_{S}^{2}}\Big[\frac{1}{S}\sum_{i}\psi_{i}\psi_{i}^{\top}+\frac{1}{S}\frac{n_{S}-1}{n-1}\sum_{i\neq j}\psi_{i}\psi_{j}^{\top}\Big]=\frac{n}{Sn_{S}^{2}}\Big(1-\frac{n_{S}-1}{n-1}\Big)\Sigma_{\psi,n}=\frac{S-1}{n-1}\Sigma_{\psi,n},

where the last step usesn=S​nSn=Sn_{S}and1−nS−1n−1=n−nSn−1=nS​(S−1)n−11-\frac{n_{S}-1}{n-1}=\frac{n-n_{S}}{n-1}=\frac{n_{S}(S-1)}{n-1}. Averaging overssleaves the right-hand side unchanged, giving𝔼π​[Σ¯ψ,n∣𝐗1:n]=S−1n−1​Σψ,n{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{S-1}{n-1}\Sigma_{\psi,n}.
∎

## Lemma S2.4.

Letξ1,…,ξK\xi_{1},\dots,\xi_{K}be independent mean-zero random vectors in a Hilbert space with𝔼​‖ξk‖4<∞{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{4}<\infty. Then𝔼​‖∑k=1Kξk‖4≤3​(∑k=1K𝔼​‖ξk‖2)2+∑k=1K𝔼​‖ξk‖4.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\Big\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\xi_{k}\Big\|^{4}\leq 3\Big(\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{2}\Big)^{2}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{4}.

## Proof.

WriteW=∑kξkW=\sum_{k}\xi_{k}. From‖W‖2=⟨W,W⟩=∑i,j⟨ξi,ξj⟩\|W\|^{2}=\langle W,W\rangle=\sum_{i,j}\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle,𝔼​‖W‖4=𝔼​[(∑i,j⟨ξi,ξj⟩)​(∑k,l⟨ξk,ξl⟩)]=∑i,j,k,l𝔼​[⟨ξi,ξj⟩​⟨ξk,ξl⟩],\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|W\|^{4}={\mathbb{E}}\Big[\Big(\sum_{i,j}\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle\Big)\Big(\sum_{k,l}\langle\xi_{k},\xi_{l}\rangle\Big)\Big]=\sum_{i,j,k,l}{\mathbb{E}}[\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle\langle\xi_{k},\xi_{l}\rangle],

a sum over the four slots(i,j,k,l)∈{1,…,K}4(i,j,k,l)\in\{1,\dots,K\}^{4}.

## Singletons vanish.

If some value occupies exactly one slot, sayiiwithj,k,l≠ij,k,l\neq i, thenξi\xi_{i}is independent of(ξj,ξk,ξl)(\xi_{j},\xi_{k},\xi_{l}), and by linearity of the inner product in its first argument,𝔼​[⟨ξi,ξj⟩​⟨ξk,ξl⟩]=𝔼​[⟨ξk,ξl⟩​⟨𝔼​ξi,ξj⟩]=0.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle\langle\xi_{k},\xi_{l}\rangle]={\mathbb{E}}\big[\langle\xi_{k},\xi_{l}\rangle\langle{\mathbb{E}}\xi_{i},\ \xi_{j}\rangle\big]=0.

## Diagonal.

The diagonal term is⟨ξi,ξi⟩2=‖ξi‖4\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{i}\rangle^{2}=\|\xi_{i}\|^{4}, contributing∑i𝔼​‖ξi‖4\sum_{i}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{i}\|^{4}.

## Two-pair patterns.

The four slots split into two equal-index pairs in exactly three ways, and each two-pair quadruple matches one of the following,{1,2}​{3,4}:i=j,k=l,i≠k∑i≠k𝔼​‖ξi‖2​𝔼​‖ξk‖2,{1,3}​{2,4}:i=k,j=l,i≠j∑i≠j𝔼​⟨ξi,ξj⟩2,{1,4}​{2,3}:i=l,j=k,i≠j∑i≠j𝔼​⟨ξi,ξj⟩2,\displaystyle\begin{array}[]{lll}\{1,2\}\{3,4\}:&i=j,\ k=l,\ i\neq k&\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq k}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{i}\|^{2}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{2},\\[4.0pt]
\{1,3\}\{2,4\}:&i=k,\ j=l,\ i\neq j&\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}{\mathbb{E}}\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle^{2},\\[4.0pt]
\{1,4\}\{2,3\}:&i=l,\ j=k,\ i\neq j&\displaystyle\sum_{i\neq j}{\mathbb{E}}\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle^{2},\end{array}

where the first line uses independence (i≠ki\neq k) to factor𝔼​‖ξi‖2​‖ξk‖2{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{i}\|^{2}\|\xi_{k}\|^{2}. Fori≠ji\neq jthe Cauchy-Schwarz inequality gives𝔼​⟨ξi,ξj⟩2≤𝔼​‖ξi‖2​𝔼​‖ξj‖2{\mathbb{E}}\langle\xi_{i},\xi_{j}\rangle^{2}\leq{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{i}\|^{2}\,{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{j}\|^{2}, so each of the three sums is at most(∑k𝔼​‖ξk‖2)2\big(\sum_{k}{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{2}\big)^{2}. Adding the diagonal and the three two-pair contributions yields the claim.
∎

## Lemma S2.5.

On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, uniformly overγ∈Γ=[γ¯,γ¯]⊂(0,∞)\gamma\in\Gamma=[\underline{\gamma},\overline{\gamma}]\subset(0,\infty),𝔼w​[‖gDirichlet‖4∣𝐗1:n]=O​(n−2),𝔼w​[‖BDirichlet‖F4∣𝐗1:n]=O​(n−2),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{w}[\|g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=O(n^{-2}),\quad{\mathbb{E}}_{w}[\|B_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|_{F}^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=O(n^{-2}),𝔼u,π​[‖gBlock‖4∣𝐗1:n]=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}),𝔼u,π​[‖BBlock‖F4∣𝐗1:n]=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}),

with constants depending only on(C,K2,dθ,γ¯)(C,K_{2},d_{\theta},\underline{\gamma})and uniform inSS.

## Proof.

Letcic_{i}denote eitherψi\psi_{i}(forgg) orAiA_{i}(forBB). In both cases∑ici=0\sum_{i}c_{i}=0on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}and‖ci‖≤C⋆:=max⁡{C,2​dθ​K2}\|c_{i}\|\leq C_{\star}:=\max\{C,2\sqrt{d_{\theta}}K_{2}\}by (A3), whereAi=∇θ2l​(Xi,θ0⋆)−H0,nA_{i}=\nabla_{\theta}^{2}l(X_{i},\theta_{0}^{\star})-H_{0,n}has‖Ai‖F≤2​dθ​K2\|A_{i}\|_{F}\leq 2\sqrt{d_{\theta}}K_{2}.

BothgDirichletg_{\text{Dirichlet}}andgBlockg_{\text{Block}}have the form of LemmaS2.2, so we may combine it with LemmaS2.4. Since𝔼​‖ξk‖2=α​‖vk‖2{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{2}=\alpha\|v_{k}\|^{2}and𝔼​‖ξk‖4=(3​α2+6​α)​‖vk‖4{\mathbb{E}}\|\xi_{k}\|^{4}=(3\alpha^{2}+6\alpha)\|v_{k}\|^{4}fromVar⁡(Ek)=α\operatorname{Var}(E_{k})=\alphaand𝔼​|Ek−α|4=3​α2+6​α{\mathbb{E}}|E_{k}-\alpha|^{4}=3\alpha^{2}+6\alpha, and using3​α2≤3​α2+6​α3\alpha^{2}\leq 3\alpha^{2}+6\alpha,𝔼​‖M‖4≤(3​α2+6​α)​[(∑k‖vk‖2)2+∑k‖vk‖4].\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}\leq(3\alpha^{2}+6\alpha)\Big[\Big(\sum_{k}\|v_{k}\|^{2}\Big)^{2}+\sum_{k}\|v_{k}\|^{4}\Big].(S2)

## org-BB case

(K=n,α=1,vi=ci,ζ=w,V=T)(K=n,\ \alpha=1,\ v_{i}=c_{i},\ \zeta=w,\ V=T). Here∑i‖ci‖2≤n​C⋆2\sum_{i}\|c_{i}\|^{2}\leq nC_{\star}^{2}and∑i‖ci‖4≤C⋆2​∑i‖ci‖2≤n​C⋆4\sum_{i}\|c_{i}\|^{4}\leq C_{\star}^{2}\sum_{i}\|c_{i}\|^{2}\leq nC_{\star}^{4}, so (S2) withα=1\alpha=1, that is3​α2+6​α=93\alpha^{2}+6\alpha=9, gives𝔼​‖M‖4≤9​[(n​C⋆2)2+n​C⋆4]=O​(n2){\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}\leq 9[(nC_{\star}^{2})^{2}+nC_{\star}^{4}]=O(n^{2}), while𝔼​[T4]=n​(n+1)​(n+2)​(n+3)≥n4{\mathbb{E}}[T^{4}]=n(n+1)(n+2)(n+3)\geq n^{4}. Hence𝔼w​[‖gDirichlet‖4∣𝐗1:n]=𝔼​‖M‖4/𝔼​[T4]=O​(n−2){\mathbb{E}}_{w}[\|g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]={\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}/{\mathbb{E}}[T^{4}]=O(n^{-2}), and likewise𝔼w​[‖BDirichlet‖F4∣𝐗1:n]=O​(n−2){\mathbb{E}}_{w}[\|B_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|_{F}^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=O(n^{-2}).

## blk-BB case

(K=S,α=γ,vs=c¯s:=1nS​∑i∈Isci,ζ=u,V=∑sEs)(K=S,\ \alpha=\gamma,\ v_{s}=\bar{c}_{s}:=\frac{1}{n_{S}}\sum_{i\in I_{s}}c_{i},\ \zeta=u,\ V=\sum_{s}E_{s}), with∑sc¯s=1nS​∑ici=0\sum_{s}\bar{c}_{s}=\frac{1}{n_{S}}\sum_{i}c_{i}=0. HeregBlockg_{\text{Block}}depends on two independent sources, the Dirichlet weightsuuand the balanced partitionπ\pi. Conditionally onπ\pi, (S2) gives𝔼u​[‖gBlock‖4∣𝐗1:n,π]=𝔼​‖M‖4𝔼​[V4]≤3​γ2+6​γ𝔼​[V4]​[(∑s‖c¯s‖2)2+∑s‖c¯s‖4].\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{u}[\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}\|M\|^{4}}{{\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]}\leq\frac{3\gamma^{2}+6\gamma}{{\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]}\Big[\Big(\sum_{s}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{2}+\sum_{s}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{4}\Big].(S3)

We invoke Hoeffding’s sampling-without-replacement argument(Hoeffding1963, Theorem 4)only through its Jensen step. Conditional on𝐗1:n{\bf X}_{1:n}the vectorsc1,…,cnc_{1},\dots,c_{n}are fixed, and Jensen applies directly to any convex scalar function of their sum. In particular, for the convex norm power used below,𝔼π​‖∑i∈Isci‖4≤𝔼​‖∑j=1nScJj‖4,J1,…,JnS​∼iid​Unif​{1,…,n},\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\Big\|\sum_{i\in I_{s}}c_{i}\Big\|^{4}\leq{\mathbb{E}}\Big\|\sum_{j=1}^{n_{S}}c_{J_{j}}\Big\|^{4},\qquad J_{1},\dots,J_{n_{S}}\overset{\mathrm{iid}}{\sim}\mathrm{Unif}\{1,\dots,n\},

wherez↦‖z‖4z\mapsto\|z\|^{4}is convex because∥⋅∥\|\cdot\|is convex andt↦t4t\mapsto t^{4}is convex nondecreasing on[0,∞)[0,\infty). The right-hand sum is i.i.d. and mean-zero with𝔼​‖cJj‖2=1n​∑i‖ci‖2≤C⋆2{\mathbb{E}}\|c_{J_{j}}\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\|c_{i}\|^{2}\leq C_{\star}^{2}and𝔼​‖cJj‖4≤C⋆4{\mathbb{E}}\|c_{J_{j}}\|^{4}\leq C_{\star}^{4}, so𝔼​‖∑jcJj‖4≤3​(nS​C⋆2)2+nS​C⋆4{\mathbb{E}}\|\sum_{j}c_{J_{j}}\|^{4}\leq 3(n_{S}C_{\star}^{2})^{2}+n_{S}C_{\star}^{4}and𝔼π​‖c¯s‖4=nS−4​𝔼π​‖∑i∈Isci‖4≤nS−4​[3​(nS​C⋆2)2+nS​C⋆4]=O​(nS−2)=O​(S2/n2).\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{4}=n_{S}^{-4}{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\Big\|\sum_{i\in I_{s}}c_{i}\Big\|^{4}\leq n_{S}^{-4}[3(n_{S}C_{\star}^{2})^{2}+n_{S}C_{\star}^{4}]=O(n_{S}^{-2})=O(S^{2}/n^{2}).

By symmetry overss,𝔼π​[∑s‖c¯s‖4]=S​𝔼π​‖c¯s‖4=O​(S3/n2),𝔼π​[(∑s‖c¯s‖2)2]≤(∑s(𝔼π​‖c¯s‖4)1/2)2=O​(S4/n2),\displaystyle\begin{gathered}{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\Big[\sum_{s}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{4}\Big]=S{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{4}=O(S^{3}/n^{2}),\\[2.0pt]
{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\Big[\Big(\sum_{s}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{2}\Big)^{2}\Big]\leq\Big(\sum_{s}({\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{4})^{1/2}\Big)^{2}=O(S^{4}/n^{2}),\end{gathered}

where the second bound follows by Minkowski’s inequality applied toYs=‖c¯s‖2Y_{s}=\|\bar{c}_{s}\|^{2}inL2​(π)L^{2}(\pi), that is‖∑sYs‖L2​(π)≤∑s‖Ys‖L2​(π)\|\sum_{s}Y_{s}\|_{L^{2}(\pi)}\leq\sum_{s}\|Y_{s}\|_{L^{2}(\pi)}, and squaring. Substituting into (S3) and taking𝔼π{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}(with𝔼​[V4]{\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]constant inπ\pi),𝔼u,π​[‖gBlock‖4∣𝐗1:n]≤3​γ2+6​γ𝔼​[V4]​[O​(S4/n2)+O​(S3/n2)].\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]\leq\frac{3\gamma^{2}+6\gamma}{{\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]}[O(S^{4}/n^{2})+O(S^{3}/n^{2})].

Finally𝔼​[V4]=S​γ​(S​γ+1)​(S​γ+2)​(S​γ+3)≥(S​γ)4{\mathbb{E}}[V^{4}]=S\gamma(S\gamma+1)(S\gamma+2)(S\gamma+3)\geq(S\gamma)^{4}, so𝔼u,π​[‖gBlock‖4∣𝐗1:n]≤3​γ2+6​γ(S​γ)4​[O​(S4/n2)+O​(S3/n2)]=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2)\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]\leq\frac{3\gamma^{2}+6\gamma}{(S\gamma)^{4}}[O(S^{4}/n^{2})+O(S^{3}/n^{2})]=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2})

uniformly overγ∈Γ\gamma\in\Gamma. The identical computation withci=Aic_{i}=A_{i}gives𝔼u,π​[‖BBlock‖F4∣𝐗1:n]{\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{4}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]the same rate.
∎

## Lemma S2.6.

Letθ0⋆:=arg⁡minθ∈Θ⁡Mn​(θ)\theta_{0}^{\star}:=\arg\min_{\theta\in\Theta}M_{n}(\theta)withMn​(θ):=L​(θ;n−1​𝟏n)=1n​∑il​(Xi,θ)M_{n}(\theta):=L(\theta;n^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{n})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}l(X_{i},\theta)andθ∞⋆:=arg⁡minθ∈Θ⁡𝔼​[l​(X,θ)]\theta^{\star}_{\infty}:=\arg\min_{\theta\in\Theta}{\mathbb{E}}[l(X,\theta)]. ## Probability of the complement (i).

For𝒢n(2)\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(2)}, apply the matrix Bernstein inequality(Tropp2012)to the i.i.d.Zi:=∇θ2l​(Xi,θ∞⋆)−H∞Z_{i}:=\nabla_{\theta}^{2}l(X_{i},\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-H_{\infty}with𝔼​Zi=0{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}=0,‖Zi‖op≤2​K2\|Z_{i}\|_{\text{op}}\leq 2K_{2}, and‖∑i𝔼​Zi2‖op≤4​K22​n\|\sum_{i}{\mathbb{E}}Z_{i}^{2}\|_{\text{op}}\leq 4K_{2}^{2}n. For𝒢n(1)\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(1)}, we haveθ0⋆∈Θ\theta_{0}^{\star}\in\Thetaalways, so𝒢n(1),c={θ0⋆∈K0}\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(1),c}=\{\theta_{0}^{\star}\in K_{0}\}withK0:={θ∈Θ:‖θ−θ∞⋆‖≥r}K_{0}:=\{\theta\in\Theta:\|\theta-\theta_{\infty}^{\star}\|\geq r\}compact, andγ0:=infθ∈K0[M​(θ)−M​(θ∞⋆)]>0\gamma_{0}:=\inf_{\theta\in K_{0}}[M(\theta)-M(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})]>0is attained onK0K_{0}becauseθ∞⋆∉K0\theta_{\infty}^{\star}\notin K_{0}is the unique minimizer of the continuousMMonΘ\Thetaby (A2). The basic inequalityMn​(θ0⋆)≤Mn​(θ∞⋆)M_{n}(\theta_{0}^{\star})\leq M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})givesM​(θ0⋆)−M​(θ∞⋆)≤|(Mn−M)​(θ∞⋆)−(Mn−M)​(θ0⋆)|M(\theta_{0}^{\star})-M(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\leq|(M_{n}-M)(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-(M_{n}-M)(\theta_{0}^{\star})|, so on𝒢n(1),c\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(1),c}, where the left side is at leastγ0\gamma_{0},𝒢n(1),c⊆{supθ∈Θ|(Mn−M)​(θ∞⋆)−(Mn−M)​(θ)|≥γ0}.\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(1),c}\subseteq\Big\{\sup_{\theta\in\Theta}\big|(M_{n}-M)(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-(M_{n}-M)(\theta)\big|\geq\gamma_{0}\Big\}.

For fixedθ\theta, the variablesYi:=l​(Xi,θ∞⋆)−l​(Xi,θ)Y_{i}:=l(X_{i},\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-l(X_{i},\theta)are i.i.d. with|Yi|≤C​DΘ|Y_{i}|\leq CD_{\Theta}. Construct anϵ\epsilon-net{θ1,…,θN}\{\theta_{1},\dots,\theta_{N}\}in which everyθ∈Θ\theta\in\Thetalies withinϵ\epsilonof someθj\theta_{j}, withN≤(3​DΘ/ϵ)dθN\leq(3D_{\Theta}/\epsilon)^{d_{\theta}}(Vershynin2018). Sinceθ↦(Mn−M)​(θ)\theta\mapsto(M_{n}-M)(\theta)is2​C2C-Lipschitz,|g​(θ)|≤|g​(θj)|+2​C​ϵ|g(\theta)|\leq|g(\theta_{j})|+2C\epsilonandsupθ|g​(θ)|≤maxj⁡|g​(θj)|+2​C​ϵ\sup_{\theta}|g(\theta)|\leq\max_{j}|g(\theta_{j})|+2C\epsilon. Choosingϵ=γ0/(8​C)\epsilon=\gamma_{0}/(8C)gives{sup|g​(θ)|≥γ0}⊆{max⁡|g​(θj)|≥3​γ0/4}\{\sup|g(\theta)|\geq\gamma_{0}\}\subseteq\{\max|g(\theta_{j})|\geq 3\gamma_{0}/4\}, and the union bound over the net att=3​γ0/4t=3\gamma_{0}/4yieldsℙ​(𝒢n(1),c)=O​(e−c′​n){\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(1),c})=O(e^{-c^{\prime}n}). ## Strong convexity and uniqueness (ii).

On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, for everyθ∈B​(θ∞⋆,r)¯\theta\in\overline{B(\theta_{\infty}^{\star},r)}theK3K_{3}-Lipschitzness ofHn​(⋅)H_{n}(\cdot)from (A3) gives‖Hn​(θ)−H∞‖op≤K3​‖θ−θ∞⋆‖+‖Hn​(θ∞⋆)−H∞‖op≤K3​r+μ∞4≤μ∞8+μ∞4<μ∞2,\displaystyle\|H_{n}(\theta)-H_{\infty}\|_{\text{op}}\leq K_{3}\|\theta-\theta_{\infty}^{\star}\|+\|H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-H_{\infty}\|_{\text{op}}\leq K_{3}r+\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{4}\leq\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{8}+\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{4}<\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{2},

so by Weyl’s inequality and (A5),Hn​(θ)⪰μ∞2​IH_{n}(\theta)\succeq\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{2}I, that is,MnM_{n}isμ∞/2\mu_{\infty}/2-strongly convex onB​(θ∞⋆,r)¯\overline{B(\theta_{\infty}^{\star},r)}. On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, a minimizer ofMnM_{n}lies in this convex ball, because the basic inequality applies to any minimizer, and a strongly convex function has at most one minimizer there, soθ0⋆\theta_{0}^{\star}is the unique minimizer. Asr≤ρ∞/4r\leq\rho_{\infty}/4, the triangle inequality givesdist​(θ0⋆,∂Θ)≥ρ∞−r≥34​ρ∞≥ρ∞/2\mathrm{dist}(\theta_{0}^{\star},\partial\Theta)\geq\rho_{\infty}-r\geq\frac{3}{4}\rho_{\infty}\geq\rho_{\infty}/2, soθ0⋆∈int​(Θ)\theta_{0}^{\star}\in\mathrm{int}(\Theta)and the interior stationarity∇θL​(θ0⋆;n−1​𝟏n)=1n​∑iψi=0\nabla_{\theta}L(\theta_{0}^{\star};n^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{n})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0holds. FinallyH0,n=Hn​(θ0⋆)⪰μ∞/2​IH_{0,n}=H_{n}(\theta_{0}^{\star})\succeq\mu_{\infty}/2\,Isinceθ0⋆∈B​(θ∞⋆,r)¯\theta_{0}^{\star}\in\overline{B(\theta_{\infty}^{\star},r)}.

## L2L^{2}rate (iii).

Split𝔼𝐗1:n​‖Δn‖2=𝔼𝐗1:n​[‖Δn‖2​𝟏𝒢n]+𝔼𝐗1:n​[‖Δn‖2​𝟏𝒢nc]{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}]+{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c}}]. On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, sincer≤ρ∞/4<ρ∞r\leq\rho_{\infty}/4<\rho_{\infty}, we haveθ0⋆∈B​(θ∞⋆,r)⊂int​(Θ)\theta_{0}^{\star}\in B(\theta_{\infty}^{\star},r)\subset\mathrm{int}(\Theta), so the empirical first-order condition∇Mn​(θ0⋆)=0\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{0}^{\star})=0holds. Applying the fundamental theorem of calculus to∇Mn\nabla M_{n}along[θ∞⋆,θ0⋆][\theta_{\infty}^{\star},\theta_{0}^{\star}]reads0=∇Mn​(θ∞⋆)+H~n​Δn0=\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})+\tilde{H}_{n}\Delta_{n}withH~n:=∫01Hn​(θ∞⋆+t​Δn)​𝑑t\tilde{H}_{n}:=\int_{0}^{1}H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star}+t\Delta_{n})\,dt. For eachtt,‖θ∞⋆+t​Δn−θ∞⋆‖=t​‖Δn‖≤r\|\theta_{\infty}^{\star}+t\Delta_{n}-\theta_{\infty}^{\star}\|=t\|\Delta_{n}\|\leq r, andHnH_{n}isK3K_{3}-Lipschitz inθ\thetaby (A3), so‖Hn​(θ∞⋆+t​Δn)−H∞‖op≤‖Hn​(θ∞⋆+t​Δn)−Hn​(θ∞⋆)‖op⏟≤K3​r⁣≤μ∞/4+‖Hn​(θ∞⋆)−H∞‖op⏟≤μ∞/4​on​𝒢n(2)≤μ∞2.\displaystyle\|H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star}+t\Delta_{n})-H_{\infty}\|_{\text{op}}\leq\underbrace{\|H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star}+t\Delta_{n})-H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\|_{\text{op}}}_{\leq K_{3}r\leq\mu_{\infty}/4}+\underbrace{\|H_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})-H_{\infty}\|_{\text{op}}}_{\leq\mu_{\infty}/4\text{ on }\mathcal{G}_{n}^{(2)}}\leq\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{2}.

By Weyl’s inequality and (A5),Hn​(θ∞⋆+t​Δn)⪰μ∞2​IH_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star}+t\Delta_{n})\succeq\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{2}I, henceH~n⪰μ∞2​I\tilde{H}_{n}\succeq\frac{\mu_{\infty}}{2}Iis invertible with‖H~n−1‖op≤2/μ∞\|\tilde{H}_{n}^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}\leq 2/\mu_{\infty}. Therefore‖Δn‖≤2μ∞​‖∇Mn​(θ∞⋆)‖\|\Delta_{n}\|\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{\infty}}\|\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\|. The score∇Mn​(θ∞⋆)=1n​∑i∇θl​(Xi,θ∞⋆)\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}\nabla_{\theta}l(X_{i},\theta_{\infty}^{\star})is an average of i.i.d. centered vectors bounded byC∞C_{\infty}, so𝔼𝐗1:n​‖∇Mn​(θ∞⋆)‖2=1n​𝔼​‖∇θl​(X,θ∞⋆)‖2≤C2n{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}\|\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\|^{2}=\frac{1}{n}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla_{\theta}l(X,\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\|^{2}\leq\frac{C^{2}}{n}, giving𝔼𝐗1:n​[‖Δn‖2​𝟏𝒢n]≤4μ∞2​𝔼𝐗1:n​‖∇Mn​(θ∞⋆)‖2≤4​C∞2μ∞2​n.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}]\leq\frac{4}{\mu_{\infty}^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}\|\nabla M_{n}(\theta_{\infty}^{\star})\|^{2}\leq\frac{4C_{\infty}^{2}}{\mu_{\infty}^{2}n}.

Off𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n},‖Δn‖≤DΘ\|\Delta_{n}\|\leq D_{\Theta}andℙ​(𝒢nc)=O​(e−c′​n){\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c})=O(e^{-c^{\prime}n}), so𝔼𝐗1:n​[‖Δn‖2​𝟏𝒢nc]=O​(e−c′​n){\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c}}]=O(e^{-c^{\prime}n}). Adding the two parts,𝔼𝐗1:n​‖Δn‖2=O​(1/n){\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}\|\Delta_{n}\|^{2}=O(1/n).

## Lemma S2.7.

Let∙∈{Block,Dirichlet}\bullet\in\{\text{Block},\text{Dirichlet}\}and recall the localization radiusr=min⁡{ρ∞4,μ∞8​K3}r=\min\{\frac{\rho_{\infty}}{4},\ \frac{\mu_{\infty}}{8K_{3}}\}. On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, conditionally on(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi), there is an eventE~n∙\tilde{E}_{n}^{\bullet}on whichθ∙⋆\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}is the unique minimizer of the weighted risk in{‖θ−θ0⋆‖≤r}\{\|\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star}\|\leq r\}andn​(θ∙⋆−θ0⋆)=−H0,n−1​n​g∙+r∙,\displaystyle\sqrt{n}(\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}-\theta_{0}^{\star})=-H_{0,n}^{-1}\sqrt{n}g_{\bullet}+r_{\bullet},

with, for the block case,ℙ​(E~nBlock​c∣𝐗1:n,π)≤82μ∞2​(S​γ+1)​(κ¯n2+σ¯ψ,n2r2)=O​(σ¯ψ,n2+κ¯n2S​γ),\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi)\leq\frac{8^{2}}{\mu_{\infty}^{2}(S\gamma+1)}\Big(\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}+\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}}{r^{2}}\Big)=O\Big(\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}+\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}}{S\gamma}\Big),𝔼​[‖rBlock‖​𝟏E~nBlock∣𝐗1:n,π]≤Φ∞​nS​γ​(σ¯ψ,n2+κ¯n2),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\text{Block}}\|\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}}}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]\leq\Phi_{\infty}\frac{\sqrt{n}}{S\gamma}(\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}+\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}),

and the per-record analog obtained by replacing(S,γ,σ¯ψ,n2,κ¯n2)(S,\gamma,\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2},\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2})with(n,1,σψ,n2,κn2)(n,1,\sigma_{\psi,n}^{2},\kappa_{n}^{2}). HereΦ∞\Phi_{\infty}is a population constant depending only onμ∞,K3,diam⁡(Θ),r\mu_{\infty},K_{3},\operatorname{diam}(\Theta),r.

## Proof.

We give the block case (∙=Block\bullet=\text{Block}, weightsw¯\bar{w}). The per-record case is identical with(S,γ,ψ¯s,A¯s,σ¯ψ,n2,κ¯n2)(S,\gamma,\bar{\psi}_{s},\bar{A}_{s},\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2},\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2})replaced by(n,1,ψi,Ai,σψ,n2,κn2)(n,1,\psi_{i},A_{i},\sigma_{\psi,n}^{2},\kappa_{n}^{2}). Throughout we work on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, whereH0,n⪰μn​IH_{0,n}\succeq\mu_{n}Iwithμn=μ∞/2\mu_{n}=\mu_{\infty}/2, the first-order condition∑iψi=0\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0holds, andB​(θ0⋆,r)¯⊂int​(Θ)\overline{B(\theta_{0}^{\star},r)}\subset\mathrm{int}(\Theta)sincer≤ρ∞/4≤12​dist​(θ0⋆,∂Θ)r\leq\rho_{\infty}/4\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{dist}(\theta_{0}^{\star},\partial\Theta)by LemmaS2.6. DefineE~nBlock:={‖BBlock‖op≤μn4}∩{‖gBlock‖≤μn​r4}.\displaystyle\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}}:=\{\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{\text{op}}\leq\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{4}\}\cap\{\|g_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\tfrac{\mu_{n}r}{4}\}.

Because∑i(w¯i−1n)=0\sum_{i}(\bar{w}_{i}-\frac{1}{n})=0on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}, we have the two boundary identitiesG​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)\displaystyle G(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=G​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)−G​(θ0⋆;n−1​𝟏n)⏟=0=∑i(w¯i−1n)​ψi=gBlock,\displaystyle=G(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})-\underbrace{G(\theta_{0}^{\star};n^{-1}\mathbf{1}_{n})}_{=0}=\sum_{i}(\bar{w}_{i}-\tfrac{1}{n})\psi_{i}=g_{\text{Block}},H​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)\displaystyle H(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=H0,n+∑i(w¯i−1n)​Ai=H0,n+BBlock.\displaystyle=H_{0,n}+\sum_{i}(\bar{w}_{i}-\tfrac{1}{n})A_{i}=H_{0,n}+B_{\text{Block}}.

## Positive definiteness on the ball.

Forθ∈B​(θ0⋆,r)¯\theta\in\overline{B(\theta_{0}^{\star},r)}, the (A3) Lipschitzness gives‖H​(θ;𝐰¯)−H​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)‖op≤K3​‖θ−θ0⋆‖≤K3​r≤μn4\|H(\theta;\bar{\bf w})-H(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})\|_{\text{op}}\leq K_{3}\|\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star}\|\leq K_{3}r\leq\frac{\mu_{n}}{4}, sincer≤μ∞/(8​K3)=μn/(4​K3)r\leq\mu_{\infty}/(8K_{3})=\mu_{n}/(4K_{3}). OnE~nBlock\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}},H​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)=H0,n+BBlock⪰μn​I−μn4​IH(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=H_{0,n}+B_{\text{Block}}\succeq\mu_{n}I-\frac{\mu_{n}}{4}I, so by Weyl’s inequalityH​(θ;𝐰¯)⪰μn​I−μn4​I−μn4​I=μn2​I,θ∈B​(θ0⋆,r)¯.\displaystyle H(\theta;\bar{\bf w})\succeq\mu_{n}I-\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{4}I-\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{4}I=\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{2}I,\qquad\theta\in\overline{B(\theta_{0}^{\star},r)}.

ThusL​(⋅;𝐰¯)L(\cdot;\bar{\bf w})isμn2\frac{\mu_{n}}{2}-strongly convex on this ball.

## The minimizer lies in the open ball.

The functionL​(⋅;𝐰¯)=∑i=1nw¯i​l​(Xi,⋅)L(\cdot;\bar{\bf w})=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bar{w}_{i}l(X_{i},\cdot)is continuous on the compact ball, so it attains a minimum there, and we show the minimum cannot occur on∂B​(θ0⋆,r)\partial B(\theta_{0}^{\star},r). Forθ∈∂B​(θ0⋆,r)\theta\in\partial B(\theta_{0}^{\star},r), the fundamental theorem of calculus and the boundary identity forG​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)G(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})giveG​(θ;𝐰¯)=gBlock+H¯θ​(θ−θ0⋆)G(\theta;\bar{\bf w})=g_{\text{Block}}+\bar{H}_{\theta}(\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star})withH¯θ:=∫01H​(θ0⋆+t​(θ−θ0⋆);𝐰¯)​𝑑t⪰μn2​I\bar{H}_{\theta}:=\int_{0}^{1}H(\theta_{0}^{\star}+t(\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star});\bar{\bf w})\,dt\succeq\frac{\mu_{n}}{2}Iby the positive-definiteness bound above. Taking the inner product withθ−θ0⋆\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star}(of normrr) and using Cauchy-Schwarz and‖gBlock‖≤μn​r4\|g_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{\mu_{n}r}{4},(θ−θ0⋆)⊤​G​(θ;𝐰¯)≥−r​‖gBlock‖+μn2​r2≥−μn​r24+μn2​r2=μn​r24>0.\displaystyle(\theta-\theta_{0}^{\star})^{\top}G(\theta;\bar{\bf w})\geq-r\|g_{\text{Block}}\|+\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{2}r^{2}\geq-\tfrac{\mu_{n}r^{2}}{4}+\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{2}r^{2}=\tfrac{\mu_{n}r^{2}}{4}>0.

The outward radial derivative is strictly positive on the whole sphere, so the minimum lies in the open ball. Call itθBlock⋆\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}, a stationary point withG​(θBlock⋆;𝐰¯)=0G(\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=0. By (A4) eachl​(Xi,⋅)l(X_{i},\cdot)is convex, soL​(⋅;𝐰¯)L(\cdot;\bar{\bf w})is convex onΘ\Thetaand this interior stationary point is a global minimizer.

## Linearization.

DefineΔBlock:=θBlock⋆−θ0⋆\Delta_{\text{Block}}:=\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}-\theta_{0}^{\star}andHBlock♯:=∫01H​(θ0⋆+t​ΔBlock;𝐰¯)​𝑑tH_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}:=\int_{0}^{1}H(\theta_{0}^{\star}+t\Delta_{\text{Block}};\bar{\bf w})\,dt. Recall from the boundary identity thatG​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)=∑i(w¯i−1n)​ψi=∑s(us−1S)​ψ¯s=gBlockG(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=\sum_{i}(\bar{w}_{i}-\frac{1}{n})\psi_{i}=\sum_{s}(u_{s}-\frac{1}{S})\bar{\psi}_{s}=g_{\text{Block}}. By the fundamental theorem of calculus,0=G​(θBlock⋆;𝐰¯)=G​(θ0⋆;𝐰¯)+HBlock♯​ΔBlock=gBlock+HBlock♯​ΔBlock.\displaystyle 0=G(\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})=G(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{\bf w})+H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}\Delta_{\text{Block}}=g_{\text{Block}}+H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}\Delta_{\text{Block}}.

ThusHBlock♯​ΔBlock=−gBlockH_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}\Delta_{\text{Block}}=-g_{\text{Block}}, and sinceλmin​(HBlock♯)≥μn2\lambda_{\min}(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})\geq\frac{\mu_{n}}{2}by integrating the positive-definiteness bound,‖ΔBlock‖≤2μn​‖gBlock‖≤r2\|\Delta_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{n}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{r}{2}. Subtracting−H0,n−1​n​gBlock-H_{0,n}^{-1}\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Block}}fromn​ΔBlock=−(HBlock♯)−1​n​gBlock\sqrt{n}\Delta_{\text{Block}}=-(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})^{-1}\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Block}}and using the resolvent identity(HBlock♯)−1−H0,n−1=−H0,n−1​(HBlock♯−H0,n)​(HBlock♯)−1(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})^{-1}-H_{0,n}^{-1}=-H_{0,n}^{-1}(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}-H_{0,n})(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})^{-1},n​ΔBlock=−H0,n−1​n​gBlock+rBlock,rBlock=n​H0,n−1​(HBlock♯−H0,n)​(HBlock♯)−1​gBlock.\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\Delta_{\text{Block}}=-H_{0,n}^{-1}\sqrt{n}g_{\text{Block}}+r_{\text{Block}},\qquad r_{\text{Block}}=\sqrt{n}H_{0,n}^{-1}(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}-H_{0,n})(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}.

WritingHBlock♯−H0,n=(HBlock♯−H​(θ0⋆;w¯))+BBlockH_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}-H_{0,n}=(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}-H(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{w}))+B_{\text{Block}}, the integral form and (A3) give‖HBlock♯−H​(θ0⋆;w¯)‖op\displaystyle\|H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp}-H(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{w})\|_{\text{op}}=‖∫01[H​(θ0⋆+t​ΔBlock;𝐰¯)−H​(θ0⋆;w¯)]​𝑑t‖op\displaystyle=\Big\|\int_{0}^{1}[H(\theta_{0}^{\star}+t\Delta_{\text{Block}};\bar{\bf w})-H(\theta_{0}^{\star};\bar{w})]dt\Big\|_{\text{op}}≤K3​‖ΔBlock‖​∫01t​𝑑t=K32​‖ΔBlock‖.\displaystyle\leq K_{3}\|\Delta_{\text{Block}}\|\int_{0}^{1}tdt=\tfrac{K_{3}}{2}\|\Delta_{\text{Block}}\|.

SinceK32​‖ΔBlock‖≤K3μn​‖gBlock‖\frac{K_{3}}{2}\|\Delta_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|and‖H0,n−1‖op,‖(HBlock♯)−1‖op≤2μn\|H_{0,n}^{-1}\|_{\text{op}},\|(H_{\text{Block}}^{\sharp})^{-1}\|_{\text{op}}\leq\frac{2}{\mu_{n}},‖rBlock‖≤4​nμn2​(‖BBlock‖op​‖gBlock‖+K3μn​‖gBlock‖2).\displaystyle\|r_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{4\sqrt{n}}{\mu_{n}^{2}}\Big(\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{\text{op}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|+\tfrac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\Big).

usingκ¯n​σ¯ψ,n≤12​(κ¯n2+σ¯ψ,n2)\bar{\kappa}_{n}\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}\leq\frac{1}{2}(\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}+\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}),S​γ+1≥S​γS\gamma+1\geq S\gamma, andΦ∞:=4μn2​(12+K3μn)\Phi_{\infty}:=\frac{4}{\mu_{n}^{2}}(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}).

By the union bound and conditional Markov,ℙ​(E~nBlock​c∣𝐗1:n,π)\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi)≤ℙ​(‖BBlock‖F>μn4)+ℙ​(‖gBlock‖>μn​r4)\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{P}}(\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}>\tfrac{\mu_{n}}{4})+{\mathbb{P}}(\|g_{\text{Block}}\|>\tfrac{\mu_{n}r}{4})≤16​κ¯n2μn2​(S​γ+1)+16​σ¯ψ,n2μn2​r2​(S​γ+1)=O​(σ¯ψ,n2+κ¯n2S​γ),\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}}{\mu_{n}^{2}(S\gamma+1)}+\frac{16\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}}{\mu_{n}^{2}r^{2}(S\gamma+1)}=O\Big(\frac{\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}+\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}}{S\gamma}\Big),

whereμn=μ∞/2\mu_{n}=\mu_{\infty}/2, again by LemmaS2.3and the boundedness ofκ¯n2,σ¯ψ,n2\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2},\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}.

By the partition mean of LemmaS2.3and the tower property,𝔼π​[σ¯ψ,n2∣𝐗1:n]=Tr​𝔼π​[Σ¯ψ,n∣𝐗1:n]=S−1n−1​σψ,n2{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\sigma}_{\psi,n}^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\text{Tr}{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{S-1}{n-1}\sigma_{\psi,n}^{2}, and the same computation withψi↦Ai\psi_{i}\mapsto A_{i}in the Frobenius geometry gives𝔼π​[κ¯n2∣𝐗1:n]=S−1n−1​κn2{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\bar{\kappa}_{n}^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{S-1}{n-1}\kappa_{n}^{2}. ## Lemma S2.8.

For𝐰∼Dirichlet​(𝟏n){\bf w}\sim\text{Dirichlet}(\mathbf{1}_{n})and𝐮∼Dirichlet​(γ​𝟏S){\bf u}\sim\text{Dirichlet}(\gamma\mathbf{1}_{S}),γ∈Γ=[γ¯,γ¯]⊂(0,∞)\gamma\in\Gamma=[\underline{\gamma},\overline{\gamma}]\subset(0,\infty), withr∙r_{\bullet}the linear approximation (S4) defined on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}(so𝔼​‖r∙‖2{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|^{2}abbreviates𝔼​[‖r∙‖2​𝟏𝒢n]{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\bullet}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}]), uniformly overγ∈Γ\gamma\in\Gamma,𝔼​‖rDirichlet‖2=O​(n−2),𝔼​‖rBlock‖2=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2).\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}=O(n^{-2}),\qquad{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}).

## Proof.

We prove the block bound. The per-record one is identical with(S,γ,gBlock,BBlock)↦(n,1,gDirichlet,BDirichlet)(S,\gamma,g_{\text{Block}},B_{\text{Block}})\mapsto(n,1,g_{\text{Dirichlet}},B_{\text{Dirichlet}})and no partition averaging. From (S5),‖rBlock‖≤4μn2​(‖BBlock‖op+K3μn​‖gBlock‖)​‖gBlock‖,\displaystyle\|r_{\text{Block}}\|\leq\frac{4}{\mu_{n}^{2}}(\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{\text{op}}+\tfrac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|)\|g_{\text{Block}}\|,

onE~nBlock\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}}, which is second order in the pair(gBlock,BBlock)(g_{\text{Block}},B_{\text{Block}}). Hence onE~nBlock\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}}, with‖BBlock‖op≤‖BBlock‖F\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{\text{op}}\leq\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F},‖rBlock‖2≤32μn4​‖BBlock‖F2​‖gBlock‖2+32​K32μn6​‖gBlock‖4,\displaystyle\|r_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\leq\frac{32}{\mu_{n}^{4}}\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{2}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}+\frac{32K_{3}^{2}}{\mu_{n}^{6}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4},

and Cauchy-Schwarz with the fourth moments of LemmaS2.5give𝔼​[‖rBlock‖2​𝟏E~nBlock]\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}}}]≤32μn4​(𝔼​‖BBlock‖F4)1/2​(𝔼​‖gBlock‖4)1/2+32​K32μn6​𝔼​‖gBlock‖4,\displaystyle\leq\frac{32}{\mu_{n}^{4}}({\mathbb{E}}\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{4})^{1/2}({\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4})^{1/2}+\frac{32K_{3}^{2}}{\mu_{n}^{6}}{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4},=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2),\displaystyle=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}),

uniformly overγ∈Γ\gamma\in\Gamma. Off the localization event,E~nBlock​c⊆{‖BBlock‖F>μn/4}∪{‖gBlock‖>μn​r/4}\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}\subseteq\{\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}>\mu_{n}/4\}\cup\{\|g_{\text{Block}}\|>\mu_{n}r/4\}, the Markov inequality and the observation‖rBlock‖≤D′\|r_{\text{Block}}\|\leq D^{\prime}leads to𝔼𝐗1:n,π​ℙ​(E~nBlock​c∣𝐗1:n,π)\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi}{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi)≤(4μn)4​𝔼​‖BBlock‖F4+(4μn​r)4​𝔼​‖gBlock‖4,\displaystyle\leq(\tfrac{4}{\mu_{n}})^{4}{\mathbb{E}}\|B_{\text{Block}}\|_{F}^{4}+(\tfrac{4}{\mu_{n}r})^{4}{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\text{Block}}\|^{4},=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2),\displaystyle=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}),

whence𝔼​[‖rBlock‖2​𝟏E~nBlock​c]≤(D′)2​𝔼𝐗1:n,π​ℙ​(E~nBlock​c∣𝐗1:n,π)=O​(γ−3​n−2)+O​(γ−3​S−1​n−2){\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}}]\leq(D^{\prime})^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi}{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{E}_{n}^{\text{Block}c}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi)=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})+O(\gamma^{-3}S^{-1}n^{-2}). Adding the two parts proves the claim.

## Leading conditional second moments.

For any deterministicMMand mean-zerogg,𝔼​‖M​g‖2=Tr​(M⊤​M​𝔼​[g​g⊤]){\mathbb{E}}\|Mg\|^{2}=\text{Tr}(M^{\top}M{\mathbb{E}}[gg^{\top}]). Applying this withM=H0,n−1M=H_{0,n}^{-1}and LemmaS2.3(𝔼w​[gDirichlet​gDirichlet⊤∣𝐗1:n]=Σψ,n/(n+1){\mathbb{E}}_{w}[g_{\text{Dirichlet}}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{\top}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\Sigma_{\psi,n}/(n+1),𝔼u​[gBlock​gBlock⊤∣𝐗1:n,π]=Σ¯ψ,n/(S​γ+1){\mathbb{E}}_{u}[g_{\text{Block}}g_{\text{Block}}^{\top}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]=\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}/(S\gamma+1)),𝔼w​[‖H0,n−1​gDirichlet‖2∣𝐗1:n]=Tr​(H0,n−2​Σψ,n)n+1,𝔼u​[‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2∣𝐗1:n,π]=Tr​(H0,n−2​Σ¯ψ,n)S​γ+1.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{w}[\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{\text{Tr}(H_{0,n}^{-2}\Sigma_{\psi,n})}{n+1},\qquad{\mathbb{E}}_{u}[\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]=\frac{\text{Tr}(H_{0,n}^{-2}\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n})}{S\gamma+1}.

In addition, taking𝔼π[⋅∣𝐗1:n]{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[\cdot\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]in the second and using the partition mean of LemmaS2.3yields𝔼u,π​[‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2∣𝐗1:n]=S−1(S​γ+1)​(n−1)​Tr​(H0,n−2​Σψ,n){\mathbb{E}}_{u,\pi}[\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{S-1}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}\text{Tr}(H_{0,n}^{-2}\Sigma_{\psi,n}). Writingan:=𝔼𝐗1:n​[𝟏𝒢n​Tr​(H0,n−2​Σψ,n)]a_{n}:={\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}\text{Tr}(H_{0,n}^{-2}\Sigma_{\psi,n})], which satisfies0≤an≤μn−2​(C)2=O​(1)0\leq a_{n}\leq\mu_{n}^{-2}(C)^{2}=O(1)withμn=μ∞/2\mu_{n}=\mu_{\infty}/2, sinceTr​(H0,n−2​Σψ,n)≤‖H0,n−2‖op​Tr​Σψ,n≤μn−2​(C)2\text{Tr}(H_{0,n}^{-2}\Sigma_{\psi,n})\leq\|H_{0,n}^{-2}\|_{\text{op}}\text{Tr}\Sigma_{\psi,n}\leq\mu_{n}^{-2}(C)^{2}on𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n},𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gDirichlet‖2=ann+1,𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2=(S−1)​an(S​γ+1)​(n−1).\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}=\frac{a_{n}}{n+1},\qquad{\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}=\frac{(S-1)a_{n}}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}.(S7)

## Conditional variance and matching.

In (S4), the observation𝔼∙​[g∙∣𝐗1:n]=0{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[g_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=0givesθ∙⋆−𝔼∙​[θ∙⋆∣𝐗1:n]=−H0,n−1​g∙+(r∙−𝔼∙​[r∙∣𝐗1:n])\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=-H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet}+(r_{\bullet}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[r_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}])and thusVar∙⁡(θ∙⋆∣𝐗1:n)=𝔼∙​[‖H0,n−1​g∙‖2∣𝐗1:n]+Var∙⁡(r∙∣𝐗1:n)−2​𝔼∙​[⟨H0,n−1​g∙,r∙−𝔼∙​[r∙∣𝐗1:n]⟩∣𝐗1:n].\displaystyle\operatorname{Var}_{\bullet}(\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})={\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet}\|^{2}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]+\operatorname{Var}_{\bullet}(r_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})-2{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[\langle H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet},r_{\bullet}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[r_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]\rangle\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}].

We then take𝔼𝐗1:n{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}to both sides. The remainder variance is controlled by LemmaS2.8via𝔼𝐗1:n​Var∙⁡(r∙∣𝐗1:n)≤𝔼​‖r∙‖2{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}\operatorname{Var}_{\bullet}(r_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})\leq{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|^{2}, and the cross term by Cauchy-Schwarz with (S7) and LemmaS2.8,|𝔼⟨H0,n−1g∙,r∙−𝔼∙[r∙∣𝐗1:n]⟩|≤𝔼​‖H0,n−1​g∙‖2𝔼​‖r∙‖2|{\mathbb{E}}\langle H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet},r_{\bullet}-{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[r_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]\rangle|\leq\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet}\|^{2}}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|^{2}}. For the per-record law𝔼​‖rDirichlet‖2=O​(n−2){\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}=O(n^{-2})and𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gDirichlet‖2=ann+1=O​(1/n){\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|^{2}=\frac{a_{n}}{n+1}=O(1/n), so both error terms areO​(n−3/2)O(n^{-3/2}). For the block law𝔼​‖rBlock‖2=O​(γ−3​n−2){\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}=O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2}), the cross term is𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2​O​(γ−3/2​n−1)\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}}O(\gamma^{-3/2}n^{-1}), and the remainder variance isO​(γ−3​n−2)O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2}). Hence𝔼𝐗1:n​[VarDirichlet⁡(θDirichlet⋆∣𝐗1:n)]=ann+1+O​(n−3/2),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\operatorname{Var}_{\text{Dirichlet}}(\theta_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})]=\frac{a_{n}}{n+1}+O(n^{-3/2}),𝔼𝐗1:n​[VarBlock⁡(θBlock⋆∣𝐗1:n)]=(S−1)​an(S​γ+1)​(n−1)+RBlock​(γ),\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n}}[\operatorname{Var}_{\text{Block}}(\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})]=\frac{(S-1)a_{n}}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}+R_{\text{Block}}(\gamma),

whereVarBlock⁡(θBlock⋆∣𝐗1:n)=Var𝐮,π⁡(θBlock⋆∣𝐗1:n)\operatorname{Var}_{\text{Block}}(\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})=\operatorname{Var}_{{\bf u},\pi}(\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n})is the joint(𝐮,π)({\bf u},\pi)variance, andRBlock​(γ):=𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2​O​(γ−3/2​n−1)+O​(γ−3​n−2)R_{\text{Block}}(\gamma):=\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}}O(\gamma^{-3/2}n^{-1})+O(\gamma^{-3}n^{-2})with𝔼​‖H0,n−1​gBlock‖2=(S−1)​an(S​γ+1)​(n−1){\mathbb{E}}\|H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\text{Block}}\|^{2}=\frac{(S-1)a_{n}}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}. ## Mean matching (anyγ>0\gamma>0).

On𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n},𝔼∙​[H0,n−1​g∙∣𝐗1:n]=H0,n−1​𝔼∙​[g∙∣𝐗1:n]=0{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[H_{0,n}^{-1}g_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=H_{0,n}^{-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{\bullet}[g_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=0, so by (S4),𝔼​[(θ∙⋆−θ0⋆)​𝟏𝒢n]=𝔼​[r∙​𝟏𝒢n]{\mathbb{E}}[(\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}-\theta_{0}^{\star})\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}]={\mathbb{E}}[r_{\bullet}\mathbf{1}_{\mathcal{G}_{n}}]and, adding theO​(e−c′​n)O(e^{-c^{\prime}n})contribution of𝒢nc\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c},‖𝔼​[θ∙⋆−θ0⋆]‖≤𝔼​‖r∙‖+O​(e−c′​n)\|{\mathbb{E}}[\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}-\theta_{0}^{\star}]\|\leq{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|+O(e^{-c^{\prime}n}). The mean is the expectation of the quadratic defect, so we bound the first moment𝔼​‖r∙‖{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|directly off (S5),‖r∙‖≤4μn2​(‖B∙‖op+K3μn​‖g∙‖)​‖g∙‖\|r_{\bullet}\|\leq\frac{4}{\mu_{n}^{2}}(\|B_{\bullet}\|_{\text{op}}+\frac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}\|g_{\bullet}\|)\|g_{\bullet}\|onE~n∙\tilde{E}_{n}^{\bullet}, using only the second moments of LemmaS2.3. After averaging the partition,𝔼​‖g∙‖2=c∙​σψ,n2{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\bullet}\|^{2}=c_{\bullet}\sigma_{\psi,n}^{2}and𝔼​‖B∙‖F2=c∙​κn2{\mathbb{E}}\|B_{\bullet}\|_{F}^{2}=c_{\bullet}\kappa_{n}^{2}withcDirichlet=1n+1,cBlock=S−1(S​γ+1)​(n−1).\displaystyle c_{\text{Dirichlet}}=\tfrac{1}{n+1},\qquad c_{\text{Block}}=\tfrac{S-1}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}.

By Cauchy-Schwarz,𝔼​[‖r∙‖​𝟏E~n∙]≤4μn2​(𝔼​‖B∙‖F2​𝔼​‖g∙‖2+K3μn​𝔼​‖g∙‖2)=O​(c∙){\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\bullet}\|\mathbf{1}_{\tilde{E}_{n}^{\bullet}}]\leq\frac{4}{\mu_{n}^{2}}(\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|B_{\bullet}\|_{F}^{2}}\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\bullet}\|^{2}}+\frac{K_{3}}{\mu_{n}}{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{\bullet}\|^{2})=O(c_{\bullet}). OffE~n∙\tilde{E}_{n}^{\bullet},‖r∙‖≤D′\|r_{\bullet}\|\leq D^{\prime}and𝔼𝐗1:n,π​ℙ​(E~n∙c)=O​(c∙){\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi}{\mathbb{P}}(\tilde{E}_{n}^{\bullet c})=O(c_{\bullet})(the second-moment localization bound of LemmaS2.7), so𝔼​‖r∙‖=O​(c∙){\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\bullet}\|=O(c_{\bullet}). Hence𝔼​‖rDirichlet‖=O​(1n+1){\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|=O(\tfrac{1}{n+1})and𝔼​‖rBlock‖=O​(cBlock){\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Block}}\|=O(c_{\text{Block}}), and by the tower property and the triangle inequality∥𝔼𝐗1:n,π[𝔼𝐮[θBlock⋆∣𝐗1:n,π]−𝔼𝐰[θDirichlet⋆∣𝐗1:n]]∥\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi}[{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf u}}[\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]-{\mathbb{E}}_{{\bf w}}[\theta_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{\star}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]]\|=‖𝔼​θBlock⋆−𝔼​θDirichlet⋆‖\displaystyle=\|{\mathbb{E}}\theta_{\text{Block}}^{\star}-{\mathbb{E}}\theta_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{\star}\|≤𝔼​‖rBlock‖+𝔼​‖rDirichlet‖,\displaystyle\leq{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Block}}\|+{\mathbb{E}}\|r_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|,=O​(S−1(S​γ+1)​(n−1)+1n+1).\displaystyle=O\Big(\tfrac{S-1}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}+\tfrac{1}{n+1}\Big).

SinceS−1S​γ+1≤1γ\frac{S-1}{S\gamma+1}\leq\frac{1}{\gamma}, this isO​(n−1)O(n^{-1})for every fixedγ>0\gamma>0, uniformly inSS. ∎

## S2.1.3Proof of Theorem4.1

We first introduce a known theoretical result ofChenet al.(2010)that quantifies the finite approximation error between a sum-type random variable and a standard Gaussian variable in distribution.

## Theorem S2.1.

Letζ1,…,ζK\zeta_{1},\dots,\zeta_{K}be independent, mean-zero random variables with∑k=1KVar⁡(ζk)=1\sum_{k=1}^{K}\operatorname{Var}(\zeta_{k})=1. LetFFbe the distribution function of∑k=1Kζk\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{k}andΦ\Phithat of𝒩​(0,1)\mathcal{N}(0,1). Then‖F−Φ‖1≤∑k=1KB​(ζk)​𝔼​|ζk|3,\displaystyle\|F-\Phi\|_{1}\ \leq\ \sum_{k=1}^{K}B(\zeta_{k}){\mathbb{E}}|\zeta_{k}|^{3},

where the constant factorB​(X)=2​Var⁡(X)​‖ℒ​(X∗)−ℒ​(X)‖1𝔼​|X3|B(X)=\tfrac{2\operatorname{Var}(X)\|{\cal L}(X^{*})-{\cal L}(X)\|_{1}}{{\mathbb{E}}|X^{3}|}andX∗X^{*}is the zero-bias version ofXX. NoteB​(X)B(X)is scale-invariant, i.e.B​(a​ζ)=B​(ζ)B(a\zeta)=B(\zeta)fora≠0a\neq 0. In one dimension‖F−Φ‖1=W1​(F,Φ)\|F-\Phi\|_{1}=W_{1}(F,\Phi), so the left-hand side is the 1-Wasserstein distance.

We apply this bound to the projected linearized bootstrap laws.

Note∑iξia=ba⊤​∑iψi=0\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{a}=b_{a}^{\top}\sum_{i}\psi_{i}=0because of the first-order condition. RecallgBlock=∑s=1S(us−1S)​ψ¯sg_{\text{Block}}=\sum_{s=1}^{S}(u_{s}-\tfrac{1}{S})\bar{\psi}_{s}andgDirichlet=∑i=1n(wi−1n)​ψig_{\text{Dirichlet}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}(w_{i}-\tfrac{1}{n})\psi_{i}. Denote byV∙:=H0,n−1​Cov⁡(n​g∙)​H0,n−1V_{\bullet}:=H_{0,n}^{-1}\operatorname{Cov}(\sqrt{n}g_{\bullet})H_{0,n}^{-1}the covariance of the linearized terms. With the projection vectoraa, LemmaS2.3impliesvDirichleta:=a⊤​VDirichlet​a=1n+1​∑i=1n(ξia)2,vBlocka:=a⊤​VBlock​a=nS​γ+1⋅1S​∑s=1S(ξ¯sa)2,\displaystyle v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}:=a^{\top}V_{\text{Dirichlet}}a=\frac{1}{n+1}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\xi_{i}^{a})^{2},\qquad v_{\text{Block}}^{a}:=a^{\top}V_{\text{Block}}a=\frac{n}{S\gamma+1}\cdot\frac{1}{S}\sum_{s=1}^{S}(\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a})^{2},

usinga⊤​H0,n−1=ba⊤a^{\top}H_{0,n}^{-1}=b_{a}^{\top}(symmetry) andba⊤​Σψ,n​ba=1n​∑i(ξia)2b_{a}^{\top}\Sigma_{\psi,n}b_{a}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i}(\xi_{i}^{a})^{2},ba⊤​Σ¯ψ,n​ba=1S​∑s(ξ¯sa)2b_{a}^{\top}\bar{\Sigma}_{\psi,n}b_{a}=\frac{1}{S}\sum_{s}(\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a})^{2}. We also defineρDirichleta:=∑i|ξia|3(∑i(ξia)2)3/2,ρBlocka:=∑s|ξ¯sa|3(∑s(ξ¯sa)2)3/2.\displaystyle\rho_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}:=\frac{\sum_{i}|\xi_{i}^{a}|^{3}}{\Bigl(\sum_{i}(\xi_{i}^{a})^{2}\Bigr)^{3/2}},\qquad\rho_{\text{Block}}^{a}:=\frac{\sum_{s}|\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}|^{3}}{\Bigl(\sum_{s}(\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a})^{2}\Bigr)^{3/2}}.

By LemmaS2.3and the partition mean, for everyγ>0\gamma>0,𝔼π​[vBlocka∣𝐗1:n]=nS​γ+1​S−1n−1​ba⊤​Σψ,n​ba=RS,n​(γ)​vDirichleta,\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[v_{\text{Block}}^{a}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=\frac{n}{S\gamma+1}\,\frac{S-1}{n-1}\,b_{a}^{\top}\Sigma_{\psi,n}b_{a}=R_{S,n}(\gamma)\,v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a},(S8)

whereRS,n​(γ):=(S−1)​(n+1)(S​γ+1)​(n−1)R_{S,n}(\gamma):=\frac{(S-1)(n+1)}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}, so that, withaS,n​(γ)=S−1(S​γ+1)​(n−1)−1n+1a_{S,n}(\gamma)=\tfrac{S-1}{(S\gamma+1)(n-1)}-\tfrac{1}{n+1},𝔼π​[vBlocka∣𝐗1:n]−vDirichleta=±n​v^a​|aS,n​(γ)|.\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[v_{\text{Block}}^{a}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]-v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}=\pm n\hat{v}_{a}\,|a_{S,n}(\gamma)|.(S9)

Atγ=γ⋆={S​(n+1)−2​n}/{S​(n−1)}\gamma=\gamma^{\star}=\{S(n+1)-2n\}/\{S(n-1)\}, we haveS​γ⋆+1=(S−1)​(n+1)n−1S\gamma^{\star}+1=\frac{(S-1)(n+1)}{n-1}, henceRS,n​(γ⋆)=1R_{S,n}(\gamma^{\star})=1and𝔼π​[vBlocka∣𝐗1:n]=vDirichleta{\mathbb{E}}_{\pi}[v_{\text{Block}}^{a}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}]=v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}.

## Proof scheme.

LemmaS2.7linearizes each bootstrap solution asa⊤​n​(θ∙⋆−θ0⋆)=−n​ba⊤​g∙+Ra∙a^{\top}\sqrt{n}(\theta_{\bullet}^{\star}-\theta_{0}^{\star})=-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\bullet}+R_{a}^{\bullet}, where the projected remainderRa∙:=a⊤​r∙R_{a}^{\bullet}:=a^{\top}r_{\bullet}obeys|Ra∙|≤‖r∙‖|R_{a}^{\bullet}|\leq\|r_{\bullet}\|. Replacing each projected law by the law of its linear part, therefore, costs only the remainder. Conditionally on(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi), we observe, by definition of the 1-Wasserstein distance,W1​(ℒ∙a,𝐗1:n,π,Law​(−n​ba⊤​g∙∣𝐗1:n,π))≤𝔼​[|Ra∙|∣𝐗1:n,π]≤𝔼​[‖r∙‖∣𝐗1:n,π].\displaystyle W_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{\bullet}^{a,{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi},\ \mathrm{Law}(-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\bullet}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi))\leq{\mathbb{E}}[|R_{a}^{\bullet}|\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]\leq{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\bullet}\|\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi].(S10)

LemmaS2.7and (S6) show that the remainder decays byO​(n−1/2)O(n^{-1/2})eventually in marginalization.

By the Gamma representationwi=Gi/Yw_{i}=G_{i}/Y,us=Γs/YSu_{s}=\Gamma_{s}/Y_{S}(LemmaS2.1) and the centrings∑iξia=0\sum_{i}\xi_{i}^{a}=0,∑sξ¯sa=0\sum_{s}\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}=0, each linear part can be represented as−n​ba⊤​gDirichlet=nY​(−1n​∑i(Gi−1)​ξia),−n​ba⊤​gBlock=1YS​(−∑s(Γs−γ)​n​ξ¯sa).\displaystyle-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}=\frac{n}{Y}\Bigl(-\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i}(G_{i}-1)\xi_{i}^{a}\Bigr),-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\text{Block}}=\frac{1}{Y_{S}}\Bigl(-\sum_{s}(\Gamma_{s}-\gamma)\sqrt{n}\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}\Bigr).(S11)

The sum-type variables in the parentheses consist of independent terms, and we apply TheoremS2.1to each one.

## Lemma S2.9.

Conditional on(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi), withvDirichleta,vBlocka>0v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a},v_{\text{Block}}^{a}>0andS​γ>2S\gamma>2,W1​(Law​(−n​ba⊤​gDirichlet∣𝐗1:n),𝒩​(0,vDirichleta))≲vDirichleta​(ρDirichleta+n−1/2),\displaystyle W_{1}(\mathrm{Law}(-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\text{Dirichlet}}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}),\mathcal{N}(0,v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}))\lesssim\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}(\rho_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}+n^{-1/2}),W1​(Law​(−n​ba⊤​gBlock∣𝐗1:n,π),𝒩​(0,vBlocka))≲vBlocka​(ρBlocka+(S​γ)−1/2),\displaystyle W_{1}(\mathrm{Law}(-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\text{Block}}\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi),\mathcal{N}(0,v_{\text{Block}}^{a}))\lesssim\sqrt{v_{\text{Block}}^{a}}(\rho_{\text{Block}}^{a}+(S\gamma)^{-1/2}),

the suppressed constants being the centered-Gamma third-moment factors of TheoremS2.1.

## Proof.

We show the block case. The per-record one is identical after replacing(Γs,γ,S)(\Gamma_{s},\gamma,S)by(Gi,1,n)(G_{i},1,n). By the Gamma representation (S11), let’s define−n​ba⊤​gBlock=T/YS-\sqrt{n}b_{a}^{\top}g_{\text{Block}}=T/Y_{S}whereT:=−∑s(Γs−γ)​n​ξ¯saT:=-\sum_{s}(\Gamma_{s}-\gamma)\sqrt{n}\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}is a sum ofSSindependent mean-zero terms with varianceτ2:=Var⁡(T)=γ​n​∑s(ξ¯sa)2\tau^{2}:=\operatorname{Var}(T)=\gamma n\sum_{s}(\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a})^{2}forΓs∼Gamma​(γ,1)\Gamma_{s}\sim\mathrm{Gamma}(\gamma,1). Defineζs:=(Γs−γ)​n​ξ¯sa/τ\zeta_{s}:=(\Gamma_{s}-\gamma)\sqrt{n}\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}/\tauso that∑sVar⁡ζs=1\sum_{s}\operatorname{Var}\zeta_{s}=1. ## Proof.

The score bounds hold throughout𝒢n\mathcal{G}_{n}:‖ba‖=‖H0,n−1​a‖≤2/μ∞\|b_{a}\|=\|H_{0,n}^{-1}a\|\leq 2/\mu_{\infty}gives|ξia|≤‖ba‖​C≤Ba|\xi_{i}^{a}|\leq\|b_{a}\|C\leq B_{a}and|ξ¯sa|≤Ba|\bar{\xi}_{s}^{a}|\leq B_{a}, whencev^a≤Ba2\hat{v}_{a}\leq B_{a}^{2}. Theξia,∞\xi_{i}^{a,\infty}are i.i.d. with𝔼​(ξia,∞)2=v∞a{\mathbb{E}}(\xi_{i}^{a,\infty})^{2}=v_{\infty}^{a}and|ξia,∞|≤C/μ∞≤Ba|\xi_{i}^{a,\infty}|\leq C/\mu_{\infty}\leq B_{a}. For the plug-in error,x↦x2x\mapsto x^{2}is2​Ba2B_{a}-Lipschitz on[−Ba,Ba][-B_{a},B_{a}], so|en|≤2​Ba​κ0​Dn|e_{n}|\leq 2B_{a}\kappa_{0}D_{n}, using‖ψi−ψi∞‖≤K2​‖θ0⋆−θ∞⋆‖\|\psi_{i}-\psi_{i}^{\infty}\|\leq K_{2}\|\theta_{0}^{\star}-\theta_{\infty}^{\star}\|and‖ba−H∞−1​a‖≤μ∞−2​‖H0,n−H∞‖op\|b_{a}-H_{\infty}^{-1}a\|\leq\mu_{\infty}^{-2}\|H_{0,n}-H_{\infty}\|_{\text{op}}. On𝒢n′\mathcal{G}_{n}^{\prime},Dn≤δD_{n}\leq\delta, so|en|≤2​Ba​κ0​δ=14​v∞a|e_{n}|\leq 2B_{a}\kappa_{0}\delta=\frac{1}{4}v_{\infty}^{a}by the choice ofδ\delta. Hencev^a≥34​v∞a−14​v∞a=ca\hat{v}_{a}\geq\frac{3}{4}v_{\infty}^{a}-\frac{1}{4}v_{\infty}^{a}=c_{a}. For the probability, each of the three events in (S12) fails with probabilityO​(n−1)O(n^{-1}). The event𝒢nc\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c}hasℙ​(𝒢nc)=O​(e−c′​n){\mathbb{P}}(\mathcal{G}_{n}^{c})=O(e^{-c^{\prime}n})by LemmaS2.6(i). ## The conditional comparison.

Combining the linearization error with LemmaS2.9, conditional on(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi),W1​(ℒBlocka,𝐗1:n,π,ℒDirichleta,𝐗1:n)\displaystyle W_{1}(\mathcal{L}_{\text{Block}}^{a,{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi},\mathcal{L}_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a,{\bf X}_{1:n}})≲vBlocka​(ρBlocka+(S​γ)−1/2)⏟(BE-blk)\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\sqrt{v_{\text{Block}}^{a}}(\rho_{\text{Block}}^{a}+(S\gamma)^{-1/2})}_{\text{(BE-blk)}}(S13)+|vBlocka−vDirichleta|⏟(gap)\displaystyle+\underbrace{|\sqrt{v_{\text{Block}}^{a}}-\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}|}_{\text{(gap)}}(S14)+vDirichleta​(ρDirichleta+n−1/2)⏟(BE-dir)\displaystyle+\underbrace{\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}(\rho_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}+n^{-1/2})}_{\text{(BE-dir)}}(S15)+𝔼​[‖rBlock‖∣𝐗1:n,π]+𝔼​[‖rDirichlet‖∣𝐗1:n].\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\text{Block}}\|\mid{\bf X}_{1:n},\pi]+{\mathbb{E}}[\|r_{\text{Dirichlet}}\|\mid{\bf X}_{1:n}].(S16)

The three bracketed terms are, in order, the block Berry-Esseen error, the standard-deviation gap between the two Gaussians, and the per-record Berry-Esseen error. The last two expectations are the linearization remainders.

## Taking expectations.

It remains to integrate (S13)-(S16) over(𝐗1:n,π)({\bf X}_{1:n},\pi). We do so on the floor event𝒢n′\mathcal{G}_{n}^{\prime}of LemmaS2.11, bounding the four contributions one at a time and handling the complement𝒢n′⁣c\mathcal{G}_{n}^{\prime c}at the very end.
We work underS​γ≥3S\gamma\geq 3, which is what LemmaS2.9requires. Note thatS​γ⋆+1=(S−1)​(n+1)n−1S\gamma^{\star}+1=\frac{(S-1)(n+1)}{n-1}givesS​γ⋆>3S\gamma^{\star}>3for everyS≥5S\geq 5andn>1n>1, so the calibrated choice always satisfies it.

## Bounding (S15).

Observe∑i|ξia|3≤Ba​∑i(ξia)2\sum_{i}|\xi_{i}^{a}|^{3}\leq B_{a}\sum_{i}(\xi_{i}^{a})^{2}andvDirichleta≤Ba\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}\leq B_{a}, whence on𝒢n′\mathcal{G}_{n}^{\prime}vDirichleta​ρDirichleta=∑i|ξia|3n+1​∑i(ξia)2≤Ban+1,vDirichleta​n−1/2≤Ba​n−1/2,\displaystyle\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}\rho_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}=\frac{\sum_{i}|\xi_{i}^{a}|^{3}}{\sqrt{n+1}\sum_{i}(\xi_{i}^{a})^{2}}\leq\frac{B_{a}}{\sqrt{n+1}},\qquad\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}n^{-1/2}\leq B_{a}n^{-1/2},

and the per-record Berry-Esseen contributionvDirichleta​(ρDirichleta+n−1/2)\sqrt{v_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}}(\rho_{\text{Dirichlet}}^{a}+n^{-1/2})is≲n−1/2\lesssim n^{-1/2}.

## Bounding (S13).

Fix𝐗1:n{\bf X}_{1:n}on𝒢n′\mathcal{G}_{n}^{\prime}, so the scoresξia\xi_{i}^{a}are fixed, centered, and bounded byBaB_{a}, and viewξ¯1a=1nS​∑i∈I1ξia\bar{\xi}_{1}^{a}=\frac{1}{n_{S}}\sum_{i\in I_{1}}\xi_{i}^{a}as the mean of a size-nSn_{S}sample drawn without replacement from{ξia}\{\xi_{i}^{a}\}. whereΔ:=w¯n​l′​(xn,g)=w​l′​(xn,g),Δ′:=w¯n​l′​(xn′,g)=w​l′​(xn′,g),\displaystyle\Delta:=\bar{w}_{n}l^{\prime}(x_{n},g)=wl^{\prime}(x_{n},g),\qquad\Delta^{\prime}:=\bar{w}_{n}l^{\prime}(x_{n}^{\prime},g)=wl^{\prime}(x_{n}^{\prime},g),

## Joint-convexity Jensen step.

The Hessian offλ​(x,y)=xλ​y1−λf_{\lambda}(x,y)=x^{\lambda}y^{1-\lambda}on(0,∞)2(0,\infty)^{2}has determinant zero and positive trace, so it is positive semidefinite. ## Beta-moment Taylor building block.

Everyww-dependence in (S2.2.2) reduces to expectations of the formgℓ:=𝔼w¯​[exp⁡(βℓ​w¯2)],βℓ:=ℓ​(ℓ−1)​C22​σ2,ℓ≥2,\displaystyle g_{\ell}:={\mathbb{E}}_{\bar{w}}[\exp(\beta_{\ell}\bar{w}^{2})],\qquad\beta_{\ell}:=\frac{\ell(\ell-1)C^{2}}{2\sigma^{2}},\qquad\ell\geq 2,(S28)

either directly (throughΦλ\Phi_{\lambda}and theMkM_{k}’s inF~,E~\tilde{F},\tilde{E}) or after Cauchy-Schwarz for oddkk. Expanding the exponential, applying termwise the Beta-moment formula𝔼​[w¯2​j]=(γ)2​j/((S​γ+α)2​j​nS2​j){\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{2j}]=(\gamma)_{2j}/((S\gamma+\alpha)_{2j}\,n_{S}^{2j})(dominated convergence is satisfied becausew¯≤1/nS\bar{w}\leq 1/n_{S}), and truncating at levelJJ,𝒯J,ℓ:=∑j=0Jβℓjj!​(γ)2​j(S​γ+α)2​j​nS2​j≤gℓ≤𝒯J,ℓ+ℛJ,ℓ,ℛJ,ℓ:=eβℓ/nS2−∑j=0J(βℓ/nS2)jj!,\displaystyle{\cal T}_{J,\ell}:=\sum_{j=0}^{J}\frac{\beta_{\ell}^{j}}{j!}\frac{(\gamma)_{2j}}{(S\gamma+\alpha)_{2j}n_{S}^{2j}}\leq g_{\ell}\leq{\cal T}_{J,\ell}+\mathcal{R}_{J,\ell},\quad\mathcal{R}_{J,\ell}:=e^{\beta_{\ell}/n_{S}^{2}}-\sum_{j=0}^{J}\frac{(\beta_{\ell}/n_{S}^{2})^{j}}{j!},

where the lower bound is the truncated series (every term non-negative) and the upper bound usesw¯≤1/nS\bar{w}\leq 1/n_{S}to dominate the tail. The two quantities𝒯J,ℓ{\cal T}_{J,\ell}andℛJ,ℓ\mathcal{R}_{J,\ell}are the only pieces of arithmetic that depend on the rationS=n/Sn_{S}=n/Sbetween the sample size and the number of blocks. Everything else in Step 2 is purely combinatorial.

## S2.2.3Step 3: composition overT​KTKreleases and conversion to(ϵ,δ)(\epsilon,\delta)-DP

By the chain rule (S18) together with theKK-fold identical Monte Carlo replication of each iteration,Aℳ​(λ)≤T​K​AJ,λ,m​(q),γMtot​(λ):=Aℳ​(λ)λ−1≤T​K​AJ,λ,m​(q)λ−1,\displaystyle A_{\mathcal{M}}(\lambda)\leq TKA_{J,\lambda,m}(q),\qquad\gamma_{M}^{\mathrm{tot}}(\lambda):=\frac{A_{\mathcal{M}}(\lambda)}{\lambda-1}\leq\frac{TKA_{J,\lambda,m}(q)}{\lambda-1},(S34)

soℳ\mathcal{M}is(λ,γMtot​(λ))(\lambda,\gamma_{M}^{\mathrm{tot}}(\lambda))-RDP under replace-one adjacency for every integerλ≥2\lambda\geq 2. Converting this RDP guarantee to(ϵ,δ)(\epsilon,\delta)-DP by the bound recalled in SectionS1.4shows thatℳ\mathcal{M}is(ϵ,δ⋆​(ϵ))(\epsilon,\delta_{\star}(\epsilon))-DP with the transparent budgetδ⋆​(ϵ)=infλ≥2exp⁡{T​K​AJ,λ,m​(q)−(λ−1)​ϵ},\displaystyle\delta_{\star}(\epsilon)=\inf_{\lambda\geq 2}\exp\{TKA_{J,\lambda,m}(q)-(\lambda-1)\epsilon\},

which is the one in Proposition2.2. The optimal conversion (S1) ofAsoodehet al.(2021), stated in SectionS1.4, only sharpens it.

We expand the per-release bound (S22) in the regimeC/(σ​nS)≪1C/(\sigma n_{S})\ll 1. Using the Taylor seriessinh⁡(z)=∑j=0∞z2​j+1/(2​j+1)!\sinh(z)=\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}z^{2j+1}/(2j+1)!atz=w¯2​C2/σ2z=\bar{w}^{2}C^{2}/\sigma^{2}and DCT withw¯≤1/nS\bar{w}\leq 1/n_{S}on the truncated Beta marginal of (S35),𝔼w¯​[Φλ​(w¯)]=2​λ​(λ−1)​∑j=0∞(C/σ)4​j+2(2​j+1)!​𝔼​[w¯4​j+2].\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\bar{w}}[\Phi_{\lambda}(\bar{w})]=2\lambda(\lambda-1)\sum_{j=0}^{\infty}\frac{(C/\sigma)^{4j+2}}{(2j+1)!}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{4j+2}].

so that𝔼w¯​[Φλ​(w¯)]=2​λ​(λ−1)​(C/σ)2​𝔼​[w¯2]​(1+RΦ​(σ,nS;C)){\mathbb{E}}_{\bar{w}}[\Phi_{\lambda}(\bar{w})]=2\lambda(\lambda-1)(C/\sigma)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{2}](1+R_{\Phi}(\sigma,n_{S};C)). The deterministic boundw¯≤1/nS\bar{w}\leq 1/n_{S}gives the moment-ratio inequality𝔼​[w¯4​j+2]𝔼​[w¯2]=𝔼​[w¯2⋅w¯4​j]𝔼​[w¯2]≤1nS4​j,j≥1,\displaystyle\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{4j+2}]}{{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{2}]}=\frac{{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{2}\cdot\bar{w}^{4j}]}{{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}^{2}]}\leq\frac{1}{n_{S}^{4j}},\qquad j\geq 1,

The geometric-tail sum on the right of (S37) admits the closed form∑j=1∞1(2​j+1)!​(Cσ​nS)4​j=∑j=1∞η2​j(2​j+1)!=sinh⁡(η)η−1withη:=C2σ2​nS2,\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{(2j+1)!}\Bigl(\frac{C}{\sigma n_{S}}\Bigr)^{4j}=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{\eta^{2j}}{(2j+1)!}=\frac{\sinh(\eta)}{\eta}-1\quad\text{with}\quad\eta:=\frac{C^{2}}{\sigma^{2}n_{S}^{2}},

obtained fromsinh⁡(z)/z=∑k≥0z2​k/(2​k+1)!\sinh(z)/z=\sum_{k\geq 0}z^{2k}/(2k+1)!atz=ηz=\etaafter subtracting thek=0k=0slot. Substituting into the definition ofRΦR_{\Phi},𝔼w¯​[Φλ​(w¯)]=2​λ​(λ−1)​C2σ2​𝔼​[w¯n2]​(1+RΦ​(σ,nS;C))=2​λ​(λ−1)​γ​(γ+1)​C2σ2​(S​γ+α)​(S​γ+α+1)​nS2​(1+RΦ​(σ,nS;C)),\displaystyle\begin{aligned} {\mathbb{E}}_{\bar{w}}[\Phi_{\lambda}(\bar{w})]&=2\lambda(\lambda-1)\frac{C^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}[\bar{w}_{n}^{2}](1+R_{\Phi}(\sigma,n_{S};C))\\

with the explicit pointwise residual0≤RΦ​(σ,nS;C)≤sinh⁡(η)η−1≤16​η2​eη.\displaystyle 0\leq R_{\Phi}(\sigma,n_{S};C)\leq\frac{\sinh(\eta)}{\eta}-1\leq\tfrac{1}{6}\eta^{2}e^{\eta}.

The last inequality follows fromsinh⁡(η)η−1=η2​∑k=0∞η2​k(2​k+3)!≤η26​∑k=0∞η2​k(2​k)!=η26​cosh⁡(η)≤η26​eη,η≥0.\displaystyle\frac{\sinh(\eta)}{\eta}-1=\eta^{2}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\eta^{2k}}{(2k+3)!}\leq\frac{\eta^{2}}{6}\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\frac{\eta^{2k}}{(2k)!}=\frac{\eta^{2}}{6}\cosh(\eta)\leq\frac{\eta^{2}}{6}e^{\eta},\qquad\eta\geq 0.

For the two-order cancellation claimed in this paragraph, it is enough to show𝖼k,0=0\mathsf{c}_{k,0}=0and𝖼k,1=0\mathsf{c}_{k,1}=0fork≥3k\geq 3, because thenMk​(w)=O​(τw2)=O​{(w​C/σ)4}M_{k}(w)=O(\tau_{w}^{2})=O\{(wC/\sigma)^{4}\}. The sharper envelope used below,Mk​(w)=O​{(w​C/σ)2​⌈k/2⌉}M_{k}(w)=O\{(wC/\sigma)^{2\lceil k/2\rceil}\}, follows from the same finite-difference identity applied to higher coefficients.

## Constant coefficient (p=0p=0).

The binomial identity∑ℓ=0k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)=(1−1)k=0\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}=(1-1)^{k}=0(k≥1k\geq 1) split intoℓ∈{0,1}\ell\in\{0,1\}andℓ∈{2,…,k}\ell\in\{2,\ldots,k\}gives∑ℓ=2k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)=(−1)k​(k−1)\sum_{\ell=2}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}=(-1)^{k}(k-1), which combined with(−1)k−1​(k−1)=−(−1)k​(k−1)(-1)^{k-1}(k-1)=-(-1)^{k}(k-1)yields𝖼k,0=(−1)k​(k−1)+(−1)k−1​(k−1)=0,k≥2.\displaystyle\mathsf{c}_{k,0}=(-1)^{k}(k-1)+(-1)^{k-1}(k-1)=0,\qquad k\geq 2.

## Linear coefficient (p=1p=1).

The factorℓ​(ℓ−1)\ell(\ell-1)vanishes atℓ=0\ell=0andℓ=1\ell=1, so the sum in (S39) atp=1p=1is unchanged by extending the range toℓ∈{0,…,k}\ell\in\{0,\ldots,k\}:𝖼k,1=∑ℓ=0k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)​ℓ​(ℓ−1)=∑ℓ=0k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)​ℓ2−∑ℓ=0k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)​ℓ.\displaystyle\mathsf{c}_{k,1}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}\ell(\ell-1)=\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}\ell^{2}-\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}\ell.

The finite-difference identity∑ℓ=0k(−1)k−ℓ​(kℓ)​ℓq=k!​S​(q,k)\sum_{\ell=0}^{k}(-1)^{k-\ell}\binom{k}{\ell}\ell^{q}=k!\,S(q,k), withS​(q,k)S(q,k)the Stirling number of the second kind, then gives𝖼k,1=k!​[S​(2,k)−S​(1,k)]=0,k≥3,\displaystyle\mathsf{c}_{k,1}=k![S(2,k)-S(1,k)]=0,\qquad k\geq 3,

sinceS​(1,k)=0S(1,k)=0fork≥2k\geq 2andS​(2,k)=0S(2,k)=0fork≥3k\geq 3.
